This setting is motivated by NISQ-era tasks such as quantum control, state preparation and variational quantum algorithms. While quantum kernels can offer a `quantum advantage' via domain-specific inductive biases, naïvely using full, high-dimensional kernels increases model complexity and information gain, leading to higher cumulative regret and poor learnability. To address this, we propose projected quantum kernels and classical kernel approximation techniques that reduce feature dimensionality while preserving key quantum properties. Using these approximate kernels, we develop misspecified GP bandit algorithms and derive regret bounds that characterize the trade-off between approximation error and information gain. The regret bounds provide principled guidance for selecting the optimal model complexity. Empirically, our methods outperform full quantum kernels in sample efficiency, while substantially reducing computational overhead, enabling scalable GP optimization for quantum-native applications.

</details>


### [77] [When Context Compensates for Sparse Event History: AlphaEarth for Spatio-Temporal Point-Process Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01082)
*Yahya Aalaila,Mouad Elhamdi,Gerrit Großmann,Daniel Jenson,Elizaveta Semenova,Sebastian Vollmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在外生空间上下文（AlphaEarth嵌入）的辅助下，时空点过程模型在事件历史稀疏时能显著提升空间迁移预测性能，尤其当历史窗口为1-2周时，性能可提升2-6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时空点过程模型在事件历史稀疏的区域难以泛化，研究旨在探索外生空间上下文能否补偿这种数据稀疏性带来的性能下降。

Method: 以固定对数高斯Cox过程为骨干，对比纯事件模型与添加AlphaEarth嵌入作为线性空间上下文的增强模型；在8个保留区域进行EMS预测的空间迁移评估，扫描不同历史窗口长度w，且仅使用锚点之前可用的AE嵌入。

Result: AE在所有历史窗口下均提升跨区域预测性能；在历史稀疏时（1-2周）提升约2-6倍，在历史充足时（w=20-104周）提升约10-20%。

Conclusion: 外生空间上下文信息能在事件历史有限时显著稳定空间迁移的点过程预测。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal point-process models must often generalise across space when local event histories are sparse. We study whether exogenous spatial context can compensate in such regimes. Using a fixed log-Gaussian Cox process backbone, we compare an event-only model with the same model augmented by AlphaEarth embeddings as linear spatial context. We evaluate spatial transfer on emergency medical services (EMS) forecasting across eight held-out regions, fixed forecast anchors, and a sweep over history length $w$, using only AlphaEarth (AE) embeddings available strictly before each anchor. AE improves out-of-region predictive performance across all history regimes, with the largest gains under scarce histories: approximately $2$--$6\times$ multiplicative improvements at $1-2$ weeks, tapering to roughly $10$--$20\%$ at $w=20$--$104$ weeks. These results show that contextual information can substantially stabilise spatially transferred point-process forecasts when event history is limited.

</details>


### [78] [SynLaD: Latent Diffusion for Generating Synthesizable Molecules Conditioned on 3D Pharmacophore Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01105)
*Miruna Cretu,John Bradshaw,Patricia Suriana,Saeed Saremi,Omar Mahmood,Kirill Shmilovich,Kangway Chuang,Vishnu Sresht,Colin Grambow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SynLaD是一个统一配体药物设计（生成什么分子）与合成可行性（如何合成）的潜在扩散框架，通过联合优化3D结构生成和合成路径规划，在生物活性配体类似物生成任务中优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有小分子生成模型通常只能优化分子活性或合成可行性中的一个目标，导致难以同时获得高评分且可合成的分子，形成了研发瓶颈。

Method: 构建编码器将分子映射到潜在空间，并设计两个解码头：几何头重建原子类型和坐标，自回归合成头输出基于反应序列的合成路径。扩散Transformer在学习的潜在空间中生成新潜在表征，并以药效团特征为条件。

Result: 在生物活性配体类似物生成任务中，SynLaD在可合成性和多样性命中生成方面优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 单个模型可以同时生成形状匹配的分子和可行的合成路线，统一了"生成什么"与"如何合成"两个目标。

Abstract: We present SynLaD, a latent diffusion framework for small-molecule generation that unifies ligand-based drug design objectives (what to make) with synthetic accessibility (how to make it). Current models typically optimize one objective at the expense of the other, creating a bottleneck for discovering high-scoring and synthesizable molecules. SynLaD combines reaction-constrained generation with pharmacophore-conditioned 3D design by learning a latent space that decodes to both 3D structures and synthesis pathways. An encoder maps molecules to a latent representation used by two decoder heads: (i) a geometric head that reconstructs atom types and coordinates and (ii) an autoregressive synthesis head that outputs synthetic routes in a serialized, reaction-based notation. A diffusion transformer generates novel latents in the learned space, conditioned on pharmacophore profiles. Across analogue generation tasks for bioactive ligands, SynLaD outperforms existing baselines in synthesizable and diverse hit generation, demonstrating that a single model can produce shape-aligned molecules with feasible synthesis plans.

</details>


### [79] [ZO-Act: Efficient Zeroth-Order Fine-Tuning via One-Shot Activation-Informed Low-Rank Subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01125)
*Xun Dong,Yibo Xu,Naigang Wang,Xin Li,Penghang Yin,Zi Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ZO-Act提出一种基于激活信息的零阶微调方法，通过将扰动限制在输入激活导出的固定低秩子空间中，降低扰动维度与估计方差，支持动量优化器和量化模型微调，在多个LLM上取得优于现有ZO方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零阶优化方法在全模型权重或随机低维子空间上进行扰动，导致高方差估计和有限性能，亟需一种更有效的低维扰动空间构造方法。

Method: 对每个线性层，在初始化时基于输入激活计算一个固定的小规模激活基，仅在该低秩子空间上优化轻量系数矩阵，通过前向损失评估实现优化；同时保持底层量化权重冻结以支持量化LLM微调。

Result: 在Llama-3-8B、OPT-13B以及INT4量化Llama-3-8B上，ZO-Act在语言理解、问答和常识推理任务中一致优于强零阶微调基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过将ZO优化限制在激活导出的低秩子空间，可同时降低方差相关的收敛项和有限差分误差，而子空间逼近偏差则被LLM激活和梯度的低秩结构所缓解。

Abstract: Zeroth-order (ZO) optimization enables fine-tuning large language models when backpropagation is unavailable or memory-prohibitive, but existing methods often perturb full model weights or randomly constructed low-dimensional subspaces, yielding high-variance estimates and limited performance. We propose ZO-Act, an activation-informed ZO fine-tuning method that restricts perturbations to a fixed low-rank subspace derived from input activations. For each linear layer, ZO-Act computes a small activation basis once at initialization and optimizes only lightweight coefficient matrices using forward-only loss evaluations. This reduces the effective perturbation dimension, exposes explicit trainable variables compatible with momentum-based optimizers such as Adam, and naturally supports quantized LLM fine-tuning by keeping low-bit weights frozen. We analyze ZO-Act as zeroth-order optimization over a restricted coefficient space and show that perturbing the low-dimensional coefficients reduces both the variance-dependent convergence term and the finite-difference error of the ZO estimator, at the cost of a controlled subspace approximation bias that is mitigated by the low-rank structure of LLM activations and gradients. Experiments on Llama-3-8B, OPT-13B, and INT4 Llama-3-8B show consistent gains over strong ZO fine-tuning baselines across language understanding, question answering, and commonsense reasoning.

</details>


### [80] [GAIA: Geometry-Adaptive Operator Learning for Forward and Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01128)
*Meenakshi Krishnan,Pranav Pulijala,Ke Chen,Haizhao Yang,Ramani Duraiswami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Geometry-Adaptive Integral Autoencoder (GAIA)，一种能够统一处理任意几何域上的正向问题（包括边界值问题）和逆问题的算子学习模型。通过几何令牌编码和交叉注意力机制，GAIA无需重新训练或迭代优化即可在单次前向传播中完成预测，在七个基准测试中，所有逆问题和BVP任务均取得最优结果，正向任务上与专用求解器性能相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何自适应神经算子主要针对输入输出共享同一空间域的正向问题设计，无法有效处理边界值问题和逆问题（输入输出可能位于不同域），限制了其在更广泛物理仿真场景中的适用性。

Method: GAIA将域边界和内部场分布编码为几何令牌，通过交叉注意力机制对积分变换层进行条件化，使核函数能够局部自适应于几何特征。该架构无需重新训练、迭代优化或图构建，即可在单次前向传播中处理任意域上的正向和逆问题。

Result: 在七个2D和3D基准测试（含四个新增/扩展的逆问题和BVP基准）上，GAIA在所有逆问题和BVP任务上取得最优结果：翼型流重建的中位数相对L2误差降低64%，EIT降低27%，在MCB所有形状类别上超越所有基线。正向问题上与专用求解器性能相当，且在对变压器基线退化的点分辨率下保持稳定精度。

Conclusion: GAIA是一种统一的几何自适应算子学习框架，有效弥合了正向问题与逆问题/边界值问题之间的方法鸿沟，为任意几何域上的大规模物理仿真提供了灵活高效的神经替代方案。

Abstract: Operator learning for partial differential equations (PDEs) on arbitrary geometries builds fast neural surrogates for large-scale simulation. Although recent geometry-adaptive neural operators have made substantial progress, they are mainly designed for forward problems in which inputs and outputs share the same spatial domain. This limits their applicability for boundary value problems (BVPs) and inverse problems, where inputs and outputs may live on different domains. We introduce the Geometry-Adaptive Integral Autoencoder (GAIA), an operator learning model that encodes the domain boundary and the interior field distribution into geometry tokens, and conditions integral transform layers on these tokens via cross-attention, allowing the kernel to adapt locally to geometric features. This yields a single architecture for forward (including BVPs) and inverse problems on arbitrary domains in one pass, without retraining, iterative optimization, or graph construction. We evaluate GAIA on seven 2D and 3D benchmarks, four of which are new or substantially extended benchmarks for inverse problems and BVP: electrical impedance tomography, optical tomography, 3D Darcy flow on varying geometries, and a modified setting of Poisson BVP on mechanical components benchmark (MCB). GAIA sets new state-of-the-art results on every inverse and BVP task, reducing median relative $L^2$ error by 64% on airfoil flow reconstruction and 27% on EIT relative to the next best amortized method, and outperforming all baselines on every shape category of MCB. On other forward problems, GAIA is competitive with specialized solvers while maintaining stable accuracy across point resolutions on which transformer-based baselines degrade.

</details>


### [81] [A Lightweight Self-Supervised Learning Framework for Multivariate Time Series using Hierarchical-JEPA on ECG Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01145)
*Siwon Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ER-JEPA，一种轻量级自监督学习框架，用于多变量时间序列（如12导联ECG）分析。其灵感源于心脏病专家的诊断方法，采用两级层次化结构，将两个联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）级联，形成层次化JEPA（H-JEPA）。该模型在约18万条10秒ECG记录上预训练，在ST-MEM基准上达到SOTA性能，且计算速度快、资源消耗低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗领域常面临目标数据集有限而大量未标注数据分布广泛的问题，自监督学习（SSL）是有效利用大数据的手段。现有SSL方法在ECG分析中虽应用广泛，但仍需更轻量、高效的框架，且受心脏病专家诊断方式启发，希望构建能编码多层次抽象表征的模型以提升复杂任务性能。

Method: ER-JEPA采用两级层次化结构：第一级构建每个时间区间的表征，第二级将这些表征作为单变量时间序列进一步处理。核心包含两个级联的联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA），形成Hierarchical JEPA（H-JEPA），并以Vision Transformer（ViT）为骨干网络。这种结构借鉴了心脏病专家先看整体波形再聚焦局部事件的两阶段诊断方式。

Result: 在约18万条10秒ECG记录上预训练后，模型在ST-MEM基准测试中达到最先进的（SOTA）下游任务性能，同时保持快速计算速度和极低的资源消耗。

Conclusion: ER-JEPA（H-JEPA）成功应用于12导联ECG多变量时间序列分析，验证了层次化表征在预训练阶段的敏感性，表明该轻量级SSL框架在医疗时间序列分析中具有高效性和实用性。

Abstract: Data analysis in the medical domain often encounters scenarios involving a limited target dataset and a large, unannotated dataset with a general distribution. Under such circumstances, self-supervised learning (SSL) methods are highly effective for utilizing large datasets, making them a popular choice for electrocardiogram (ECG) analysis. This work presents the Event Reconstruction Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (ER-JEPA), a lightweight SSL framework for multivariate time series, whose name and two-fold hierarchical structure are inspired by the diagnostic approach of cardiologists. At its core, ER-JEPA features: (1) a two-stage structure that constructs representations for each time interval and subsequently processes these representations as a univariate time series, (2) the hierarchical integration of two Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs), and (3) a Vision Transformer (ViT) backbone. The structural concatenation of two JEPAs categorizes the model as a Hierarchical JEPA (H-JEPA), designed to encode multiple levels of abstract representations for enhanced prediction on complex tasks. This study reports a successful application of H-JEPA to 12-lead ECG data as a multivariate time series alongside an analysis of the sensitivity of hierarchical representation during the pretraining stage. Pretrained on approximately 180,000 10-second recordings, the model achieves state-of-the-art downstream performance on the ST-MEM benchmark, with rapid computation and minimal resource usage.

</details>


### [82] [Efficient Compression of Structured and Unstructured Volumes via Learned 3D Gaussian Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01164)
*Landon Dyken,Sharmistha Chakrabarti,Nathan Debardeleben,Steve Petruzza,Qi Wu,Will Usher,Sidharth Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于3D高斯基元的显式体数据压缩模型，通过将3D高斯集合重新解释为标量场表示，结合CUDA加速采样和误差引导的稠密化策略，在无需网格存储的情况下实现了对结构化和非结构化体数据的高效压缩，相比隐式神经表示方法在非结构化数据上全面超越，并在结构化数据上达到竞争性重建质量与显著训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐式神经表示（INRs）在压缩非结构化体数据时无法编码几何信息，需要额外存储部分网格用于后续采样，限制了压缩比；同时需要探索能同时编码几何与标量场且压缩效率更高的显式表示方法。

Method: 将3D高斯基元集合重新解释为标量场的显式表示，通过空间位置处与相交高斯的加权聚合重建标量值；开发了针对结构化和非结构化数据的CUDA加速采样管线；设计了促进准确域编码的损失函数，以及基于采样误差的稠密化策略。

Result: 在结构化体数据上，该方法达到与INRs竞争的重建质量并实现显著训练加速；在非结构化体数据上，该方法在所有评估指标上均显著优于现有INRs。

Conclusion: 显式的3D高斯模型天然编码域几何结构，消除了非结构化体数据中网格存储的需求，带来了更高的压缩机会，是一种有效的体数据压缩新范式。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that implicit neural representations (INRs) can be trained to effectively compress structured and unstructured volume data, allowing for direct data querying with a reduced memory footprint. However, as existing INRs for unstructured volumes do not encode geometry, they require partial mesh storage for later sampling, limiting achievable compression. At the same time, novel view synthesis methods have shown that explicit collections of 3D Gaussians can be used to accurately visualize volume data. In this work, we introduce an explicit model for volume data compression based on 3D Gaussian primitives. We reinterpret collections of 3D Gaussians as an explicit representation of a scalar field and use a sampling strategy that reconstructs scalar values at spatial locations through weighted aggregation of intersecting Gaussians. We develop optimized CUDA-accelerated pipelines for structured and unstructured model sampling, loss functions that encourage accurate domain encoding by our models, and a novel sampling-error based densification strategy. Our explicit formulation naturally encodes domain geometry, eliminating the need for mesh storage in unstructured volumes and introducing significantly higher compression opportunities. Compared to existing INRs, we demonstrate that our explicit model achieves competitive reconstruction quality with significant training speedups on structured volumes, while markedly outperforming in all metrics on unstructured volumes.

</details>


### [83] [Decision-Aware Training for Sample-Based Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01171)
*Kornelius Raeth,Nicole Ludwig*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种决策感知训练方法，通过在能量评分目标中增加可微决策损失，使样本生成模型在训练时直接考虑下游决策成本，在保持完整概率预测的同时，显著改善了成本敏感区域的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的样本生成模型训练目标（如能量评分等严格适当的评分规则）仅根据数据密度分配训练信号，对决策者的成本结构完全无感知，导致在成本敏感区域预测误差高昂时模型无法针对性优化。

Method: 在能量评分目标函数中引入可微决策损失项，构成联合损失函数进行训练；决策损失直接惩罚基于模型预测做出决策时产生的成本，且理论上被证明其本身也是一个适当的评分规则。

Result: 在一个合成任务和两个真实世界任务上验证，该方法在保持完整概率预测能力的同时，在成本敏感区域实现了针对性的性能提升。

Conclusion: 决策感知训练能够有效桥接概率预测模型训练与下游决策成本之间的鸿沟，在不牺牲预测完整性的前提下优化实际决策效用。

Abstract: Sample-based generative models are increasingly used for probabilistic forecasting in high-stakes decision settings, yet their training objectives are blind to the decision maker's cost structure. These models are commonly trained with strictly proper scoring rules, such as the energy score, which allocate their training signal in proportion to data density, with no awareness of where forecast errors are most costly for downstream decisions. We therefore propose decision-aware training for sample-based generative models, augmenting the energy score objective with a differentiable decision loss that directly penalises the cost incurred by acting on the model's forecast. This combined loss is theoretically grounded, as the decision loss is itself a proper scoring rule. We validate our method on one synthetic and two real-world tasks, showing targeted improvements in cost-sensitive regions while retaining full probabilistic forecasts.

</details>


### [84] [QuasiMoTTo: Quasi-Monte Carlo Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01179)
*Michael Y. Li,Anthony Zhan,Kanishk Gandhi,Noah D. Goodman,Emily B. Fox*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QuasiMoTTo，通过准蒙特卡洛方法生成相关但边际分布正确的并行样本，替代传统i.i.d.采样，在推理计算扩展和强化学习中显著提升样本效率（匹配相同准确率需减少25-47%样本，RL训练步骤减半），且几乎达到边际保持采样器的理论性能上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展推理计算时，并行生成大量独立样本虽有效但存在冗余浪费。这种浪费似乎不可避免，因为独立性是并行采样易于扩展的基础。然而，独立性与并行性之间并非根本性权衡——存在一个丰富设计空间，可以生成相关但精确的并行样本，从而提升样本效率。

Method: 将自回归采样重参数化为逆CDF采样，用准蒙特卡洛（QMC）替代i.i.d.均匀分布。QMC使均匀样本在空间中分布更均匀，从而生成覆盖输出空间且冗余极少的样本。这些样本虽相关，但每个样本边际分布仍与原语言模型一致，可用于策略梯度训练。同时开发了无偏bootstrap估计器来评估相关采样器的pass@k。

Result: 在四个推理基准上，QuasiMoTTo以25-47%更少的样本匹配i.i.d.的pass@k准确率，且经常达到任何边际保持采样器的pass@k理论上限。在策略梯度RL（GRPO）中，以50%更少的训练步骤匹配i.i.d.性能。

Conclusion: 通过提高样本覆盖质量获得更强的学习信号，QuasiMoTTo在推理计算扩展和RL训练中均能显著提升样本效率，且不牺牲每个样本的边际分布正确性。

Abstract: Scaling inference compute, by generating many parallel attempts per problem, is a costly but reliable lever for improving language model capabilities. By default these attempts are generated independently, wasting inference compute on redundant solutions. This waste seems unavoidable. After all, independence is what makes parallel sampling trivial to scale. However, this tradeoff is not fundamental: there is a rich design space of samplers that generate correlated but exact samples entirely in parallel. We explore this design space as an avenue for improving sample efficiency in scaling inference compute and reinforcement learning (RL). Concretely, we introduce QuasiMoTTo, which uses correlated samples as a drop-in replacement for i.i.d. samples. To generate these samples, QuasiMoTTo uses a reparameterization of autoregressive sampling as inverse-CDF sampling and draws the underlying uniforms with quasi-Monte Carlo (QMC); because QMC spreads the uniforms out more evenly than i.i.d., the resulting samples cover the output space with far less redundancy. Even though the batch is correlated, each sample is marginally distributed according to the language model, so we can use the batch for policy-gradient training. Our empirical analysis focuses on understanding how efficiently QuasiMoTTo can turn compute into performance. To evaluate correlated samplers, whose dependence breaks standard pass@k estimators, we first develop an unbiased bootstrap estimator. Across four reasoning benchmarks, QuasiMoTTo matches i.i.d. pass@k accuracy with 25-47% fewer samples. Strikingly, QuasiMoTTo often saturates an upper bound on pass@k that holds for any marginal-preserving sampler. We also apply QuasiMoTTo to policy-gradient RL (GRPO) where it matches i.i.d. performance with 50% fewer training steps. These gains come from higher coverage, which yields a stronger learning signal per batch.

</details>


### [85] [Neural Certificate Pricing for Combinatorial Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01185)
*Jingyi Chen,Xinyuan Zhang,Xinwu Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出神经证书定价（NCP）方法，利用组合优化中解证书可多项式验证但搜索空间呈指数级增长的对称性，通过无监督学习训练神经网络预测证书级对偶价格，再经结构化恢复层构造原始边际变量，实现摊销分离。在三类组合优化问题上，NCP以更少计算时间超越或持平现有神经基线，并展现出更强的分布外泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合优化问题因离散结构的可认证性导致指数级搜索空间，但解证书的可行性可在多项式时间内验证。利用这一非对称性，设计一种无需枚举违反约束、可高效学习的无监督框架。

Method: 提出神经证书定价（NCP）：神经网络无监督学习预测证书级对偶价格；结构化恢复层根据对偶价格构造原始边际变量；当证书一致性条件满足时，恢复的边际变量全局可行；理论证明预测价格的一阶误差仅导致目标值的二阶损失。

Result: 在三类组合优化问题上，NCP大幅超越或持平最先进神经基线，且计算时间更少；展现出更强的分布外泛化能力。

Conclusion: NCP利用组合优化的可验证性非对称性，通过摊销分离策略有效避免了枚举不等式约束，兼具理论保证与实证优势。

Abstract: Combinatorial optimization (CO) problems are difficult because certifiable discrete structure induces exponential search. One needs to search over the set exponentially many candidates to certify optimality, however, the structural feasibility of a path, packing, or cover can be verified in polynomial time once supplied. In this study, we introduce Neural Certificate Pricing (NCP) that exploits this asymmetry under an unsupervised learning framework. A neural network is trained to predict certificate-level dual prices, while a structured recovery layer constructs the induced primal marginal. NCP can be viewed as amortized separation: instead of enumerating violated inequalities, it learns the residual prices through which their aggregate effect enters recovery. When the certificate-consistency condition holds, the recovered marginal is globally feasible, and a local theory shows that first-order errors in the predicted price induce only second-order loss in objective value. Across three classes of CO problems, NCP either outperforms state-of-the-art neural baselines by large margins or matches them at a fraction of the computation time, and shows stronger out-of-distribution generalization.

</details>


### [86] [Quantum vs. Classical Machine Learning: A Unified Empirical Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01197)
*Chuanming Yu,Jiaming Liu,Zihao Ge,Xiongfei Wu,Lulu Zhu,Pengzhan Zhao,Jianjun Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过七组模型对（涵盖监督学习和强化学习）的实证对比发现，当前量子机器学习模型在预测性能、策略稳定性和训练时间上尚未超越经典基线模型，但在噪声过滤和假阳性控制方面展现出潜力。研究总结了QML在硬件环境、训练效率和收敛稳定性方面的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有证据不足以支撑量子机器学习模型相比经典模型具有性能优势，因此需要系统的实证研究来评估QML模型的实际表现及其与经典模型的差距。

Method: 选取七组模型对（涵盖监督学习和强化学习），对QML模型与其经典对应模型进行实证对比，评估指标包括预测性能、策略稳定性、训练时间以及噪声过滤和假阳性控制能力。

Result: QML模型在整体预测性能、策略稳定性和训练时间上未超越经典基线；但在过滤噪声和控制假阳性方面表现出一定潜力。

Conclusion: QML在硬件环境、训练效率和收敛稳定性方面仍面临挑战，需要进一步研究其鲁棒性和参数优化；代码已开源。

Abstract: Quantum computing has emerged as a promising computational paradigm for machine learning (ML), with the potential to offer computational advantages over classical approaches. At this stage, the evidence supporting the performance and advantages of quantum machine learning (QML) models relative to classical models is insufficient.To address this gap, this paper presents an empirical study on the performance of QML models and their classical counterparts. We compare seven model pairs spanning supervised learning and reinforcement learning. Our results indicate that the evaluated quantum machine learning models do not yet surpass the classical baselines in overall prediction performance, policy stability, or training time. Nevertheless, QML remains a promising approach for filtering noise and controlling false positives. Our research findings summarize the challenges facing quantum machine learning across hardware environments, training efficiency, and convergence stability, providing a foundation for research into the robustness and parameter optimization of QML. This work is publicly available at https://github.com/Z-537-437/QML.

</details>


### [87] [TiRex-2: Generalizing TiRex to Multivariate Data and Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01204)
*Patrick Podest,Marco Pichler,Elias Bürger,Levente Zólyomi,Bernhard Voggenberger,Wilhelm Berghammer,Daniel Klotz,Sebastian Böck,Günter Klambauer,Sepp Hochreiter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TiRex-2 是一种基于递归 xLSTM 的时间序列基础模型，支持多元预测和协变量处理，以恒定推理成本实现流式处理，在零样本任务上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的时间序列预测中，观测值持续到达、变量联合演化且部分协变量未来已知；现有 Transformer 基础模型在流式场景下因二次复杂度和全历史重计算而效率低下。

Method: 采用基于 xLSTM 的循环架构，包含双向时间混合器和非对称分组注意力变量混合器，支持未来已知协变量与严格因果约束；提出合成耦合流水线从大规模单变量语料构建多元训练样本。

Result: 在 GIFT-Eval 和 fev-bench 上零样本性能达到 SOTA，支持任意上下文长度的稳定流式推理，单变量模式 38.4M 参数，多元模式额外 44.1M 参数，推理成本恒定为每 patch 常数。

Conclusion: TiRex-2 是首个同时具备循环记忆、多元协变量融合、流式恒定成本特性的时间序列基础模型，为实际高频、流式预测任务提供了高效可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce TiRex-2, a recurrent xLSTM-based time series foundation model that generalizes the univariate TiRex to multivariate forecasting with both past and future covariates. Real-world forecasting is inherently sequential: observations arrive continuously, variables evolve jointly, and a subset of covariates is known ahead of time. Existing Transformer-based time series foundation models capture cross-variate dependencies but incur quadratic complexity in context length and require full-history recomputation as new observations arrive. TiRex-2 addresses these limitations through a memory-centric recurrent design that operates at constant per-patch cost under streaming. The model combines a bidirectional time mixer with an asymmetric grouped-attention variate mixer, enabling the integration of future-known covariates while preserving strict causality over target variables. To our knowledge, this is the first time series foundation model that achieves this combination of properties. To support scalable multivariate pretraining, we propose a synthetic coupling pipeline that composes diverse multivariate samples on the fly from large univariate corpora. Empirically, TiRex-2 achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot performance on GIFT-Eval and fev-bench, remains stable when streamed to arbitrary context lengths, and maintains constant inference cost per patch. The model uses 38.4M active parameters in univariate mode, with an additional 44.1M parameters activated for multivariate forecasting.

</details>


### [88] [Is One Layer Enough? Training A Single Transformer Layer Can Match Full-Parameter RL Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01232)
*Zijian Zhang,Rizhen Hu,Athanasios Glentis,Dawei Li,Chung-Yiu Yau,Hongzhou Lin,Mingyi Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了RL后训练对LLM各Transformer层的贡献分布，发现单个中间层即可恢复甚至超越全参数RL训练的收益，RL收益高度集中在少数中间层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RL后训练方法通常均匀更新所有模型参数，隐含假设各层对RL收益贡献相同。本文旨在挑战这一假设，通过逐层分析揭示RL适应在Transformer层间的实际分布。

Method: 引入"层贡献度"指标，量化单层训练恢复全参数RL改进的比例。在Qwen3/Qwen2.5两个模型家族、GRPO/GiGPO/Dr.GRPO三种RL算法、数学推理/代码生成/智能体决策等多个任务域上进行系统比较。

Result: RL收益高度集中在少数甚至单个Transformer层；高贡献层位于Transformer堆叠的中部，而输入和输出端层贡献显著较低；层排名在不同数据集、任务、模型家族和RL算法间保持强相关性。

Conclusion: RL训练对LLM的适应并非均匀分布，而是集中在中间层；这一结构性模式具有跨模型、算法和任务的稳定性和普适性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a central component of post-training large language models (LLMs), yet little is understood about how RL adaptation is distributed across transformer layers. Existing approaches typically update all model parameters uniformly, implicitly assuming that every layer contributes similarly to the gains obtained during RL post-training. In this work, we challenge this assumption through a systematic layer-wise study of RL training. Surprisingly, we find that training a single transformer layer can recover most of the gains achieved by full-parameter RL training, and in some cases even surpass it. To quantify this phenomenon, we introduce the quantity layer contribution, which measures the fraction of full RL improvement recovered by training a layer in isolation. Across seven models spanning two model families (Qwen3, Qwen2.5), three RL algorithms (GRPO, GiGPO, Dr. GRPO), and multiple task domains including mathematical reasoning, code generation, and agentic decision-making, we observe a remarkably stable pattern: RL gains are highly concentrated in a small subset of, and in many cases even a single, transformer layers. More strikingly, the same structural pattern consistently emerges: high-contribution layers concentrate in the middle of the transformer stack, while layers near the input and output ends contribute substantially less. The resulting layer rankings remain strongly correlated across datasets, tasks, model families, and RL algorithms.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [89] [SmoothAgent: Efficient Long-Horizon LLM-Based Agent Serving with Lookahead Context Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00151)
*Zaifeng Pan,Qianxu Wang,Zhengding Hu,Chang Chen,Yue Guan,Yanbo Zhou,Steven Swanson,Yufei Ding*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种前瞻性编程模型，利用上下文转换的段可分解性，异步预执行转换并预缓存KV缓存，从而消除LLM智能体系统中上下文转换带来的TTFT延迟，最高提升11.9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体执行多轮工作流时，上下文不断增长，现代智能体框架采用上下文工程策略（卸载、缩减、隔离）来控制上下文长度，但这些策略引入的上下文转换开销会导致每次转换失效已有KV缓存并触发重新预填充，显著增加了首token生成时间（TTFT）。

Method: 1. 识别上下文转换的"段可分解性"——前缀的转换独立于后续token；2. 提出前瞻性编程模型，允许智能体框架以异步操作表达上下文转换，不修改执行逻辑；3. 运行时主动预执行转换并预生成KV缓存，实现无阻塞的上下文替换；4. 设计前瞻性感知调度器，在LLM服务系统中支持异步请求与延迟敏感负载共存并控制干扰。

Result: 实现了对代表性上下文工程策略的支持，并集成到现有智能体框架和LLM服务系统中。实验表明该方法有效消除了转换开销，TTFT最多降低11.9倍。

Conclusion: 利用上下文转换的段可分解性，通过异步预执行和缓存机制，可以在不改变原有执行逻辑的情况下显著降低上下文工程策略带来的性能开销，为构建更高效的LLM智能体系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: LLM-based agents execute multi-turn workflows with continuously growing contexts, where LLM calls are interleaved with tool invocations and environment feedback. To maintain model quality, modern agent frameworks rely on context engineering strategies such as offloading, reduction, and isolation to control the context length. However, these strategies introduce significant context transformation overhead: each transformation invalidates existing KV caches and triggers re-prefill, leading to increased time-to-first-token (TTFT).
  In this paper, we identify that context transformations are segment-decomposable, where the transformation of a prefix is independent of future tokens. This property enables transformations to be executed ahead of time. Based on this insight, we propose a lookahead programming model that allows agent frameworks to express context transformations as asynchronous operations without modifying their execution logic. The runtime proactively executes these transformations and prepares transformed KV caches in advance, enabling direct context replacement without blocking. We further design a lookahead-aware scheduler in LLM serving systems to support these asynchronous requests alongside latency-critical workloads with controlled interference. We implement our approach to support representative context engineering strategies and integrate it into existing agent frameworks and LLM serving systems. Experiments show that our approach effectively eliminates transformation overhead and reduces TTFT by up to 11.9x.

</details>


### [90] [Promise-Future Synchronization for Cluster Asynchronous Many-Task Runtimes via MPI One-Sided Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00303)
*Mia Reitz*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对ItoyoriFBC AMT运行时中现有future-only模型的局限性（future与生产者绑定、读任务数需编译时固定），扩展实现了promise-future模型，支持动态依赖创建。以分层LU分解（HLU）为例进行实验，在16节点上获得近理想加速比（15.6倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ItoyoriFBC AMT运行时的future-only模型限制了动态算法的表达，因为每个future在创建时即绑定生产者，且读取该future的任务数量必须在编译时确定，无法直接表达运行时动态创建依赖关系的算法。

Method: 在ItoyoriFBC运行时中扩展实现promise-future模型，解除future-only模型的两项限制（生产者绑定与编译时固定读任务数），从而支持动态依赖创建。

Result: 以分层LU分解（HLU）作为动态算法测试用例，在16节点规模上进行实验，观察到近理想扩展，获得15.6倍加速比。

Conclusion: 通过引入promise-future模型，ItoyoriFBC运行时成功支持了动态依赖算法，且实验证明该方法具备良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Asynchronous Many-Task (AMT) runtimes use futures as placeholders for values produced by other tasks. In the ItoyoriFBC AMT runtime, the existing future-only model binds each future to its producer at creation time and requires the number of tasks that read each future to be fixed at compile time. This prevents directly expressing algorithms that create dependencies dynamically. We extend ItoyoriFBC with an implementation of a promise-future model that lifts these limitations. Thereby, our ItoyoriFBC variant supports dynamic algorithms such as Hierarchical LU factorization (HLU). We experimentally evaluated our implementation using HLU on up to 16 nodes and observed near-ideal scaling with a 15.6x speedup.

</details>


### [91] [CloudyGUI: A Novel Python-based Framework for Auto-Scaling and Cloud Workload Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00455)
*Jyoti Bawa,Mohit Kaushik,Kuljit Kaur Chahal,Kamaljit Kaur*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: CloudyGUI是一个带图形界面的Python仿真框架，通过工作负载生成、预测（XGBoost和LSTM）和基于MAPE循环的自动缩放三阶段流水线，为云环境资源管理策略提供测试验证平台。实验表明生成工作负载与真实数据高度匹配（K-S检验p值0.19/0.14），GUI开销仅为命令行工具的1.4-4.67倍，经专家评审确认其实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云环境高度动态，资源管理面临重大挑战，现有缺乏易用的仿真平台来测试和验证资源管理策略。

Method: 采用三阶段流水线：工作负载生成、基于XGBoost和LSTM的预测、基于MAPE循环的自动缩放系统；通过内部、中间和外部三种验证方法确保系统可靠性。

Result: 生成工作负载与真实数据集高度一致，CPU和内存的K-S检验p值分别为0.19和0.14；GUI相比命令行工具仅增加1.4-4.67倍开销；专家评审认可其真实性和实用性。

Conclusion: CloudyGUI填补了云应用自动缩放仿真平台的可访问性和效率方面的关键空白，有助于研究人员开发先进的云管理解决方案。

Abstract: Purpose: Cloud computing environments are highly dynamic, creating major challenges for resource management. Accurate workload prediction is therefore essential for effective auto-scaling. To address this, we present CloudyGUI, a Python simulation framework with an easy-to-use GUI that allows researchers to test and validate resource management strategies. Methods: This framework employs a three-stage pipeline: workload generation, prediction (utilizing XGBoost and LSTM), and an auto-scaling system based on the MAPE loop. Validation includes internal, intermediate, and external methods to ensure system reliability. Results: CloudyGUI's generated workloads closely match real-world datasets. A two-sample K-S test confirms this alignment, showing strong p-values of 0.19 for CPU and 0.14 for memory. When compared to a command-line tool, the GUI adds only a minimal overhead of 1.4x-4.67x. Furthermore, expert review validates the tool's realism and practical usefulness. Conclusion: CloudyGUI fills a critical gap by providing an accessible and efficient platform for simulating auto-scaling in cloud applications, helping researchers develop advanced cloud management solutions.

</details>


### [92] [ELDR: Expert-Locality-Aware Decode Routing for PD-Disaggregated MoE Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00466)
*Sangjin Choi,Sukmin Cho,Yifan Xiong,Ziyue Yang,Youngjin Kwon,Peng Cheng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ELDR是一种面向PD分离式MoE LLM服务的专家局部性感知解码路由器，通过预填充阶段的专家签名预测生成阶段的专家激活模式，结合平衡K-means聚类和局部性感知路由，在高达40 GPU的部署中实现了5.9-13.9%的TPOT中位数降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的PD分离式解码路由器仅考虑负载均衡，但MoE模型中即使负载均衡的工作节点也可能因不同请求批次激活不同专家而导致推理延迟差异，因此需要一种考虑专家局部性的路由策略。

Method: 1) 利用请求预填充阶段激活的专家构建专家签名，预测生成阶段将激活的专家；2) 离线使用平衡K-means将签名空间划分到各个解码工作节点；3) 在线采用局部性感知带路由，将请求发送到与其签名最匹配的工作节点中负载最轻的那个；4) 设计签名缓存，与KV缓存协同索引，在KV块粒度下保持前缀缓存的签名一致性。

Result: 在vLLM中实现，在最高40 GPU的部署上，相比四种负载均衡基线中最强的方案，在三种MoE模型和两种工作负载下，ELDR将中位数TPOT降低了5.9-13.9%，且模型输出不变。

Conclusion: ELDR通过引入专家局部性感知路由，有效解决了PD分离式MoE服务中负载均衡但延迟不均衡的问题，显著提升了服务性能。

Abstract: In prefill-decode (PD) disaggregated LLM serving, each request is assigned to a decode worker after prefill. Existing decode routers balance only load; for mixture-of-experts (MoE) models this is incomplete: equally loaded workers can differ in latency, since each decode step loads the weights of every distinct expert its batch activates. We present ELDR, an expert-locality-aware decode router for PD-disaggregated MoE serving. From a request's prefill expert activations, ELDR builds an expert signature predicting the experts it will activate during generation. Offline, balanced K-means partitions signature space across decode workers; online, locality-band routing sends each request to the least-loaded worker among those best matching its signature. A signature cache, co-indexed with the KV cache at KV-block granularity, keeps signatures exact under prefix caching. Implemented in vLLM and evaluated on deployments of up to 40 GPUs, ELDR reduces median TPOT by 5.9-13.9% over the strongest of four load-balancing baselines across three MoE models and two workloads, with model outputs unchanged.

</details>


### [93] [Modeling and Chasing the Energy-Efficiency Sweet Spots in Modern GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00819)
*Ayesha Afzal,Markus Manfred Li,Michael Panzlaff*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了异构CPU-GPU系统中硬件配置（频率缩放和功率上限）对能量效率的影响，通过分子动力学基准测试（GROMACS、AMBER）和压力测试基准（FIRESTARTER）在A40、A100、H100、H200 GPU和Intel Ice Lake CPU平台上实验。发现能量效率存在工作负载和架构依赖的相变，由非线性GPU功率-频率缩放驱动。作者提出一个可解释的分析模型，将GPU功率分解为线性和非线性分量，识别出一个过渡频率，超过该频率效率下降。模型拟合误差低，指出基线功率、非线性功率行为和过渡频率是主导参数。功率上限通常不如频率降低有效。研究为高效HPC执行提供了模型驱动的操作点选择指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能效是异构CPU-GPU高性能计算的关键限制，现有研究缺乏对硬件配置（频率缩放、功率上限）如何在实际工作负载下影响能量-解决方案的深入理解和可预测模型。

Method: 在多种GPU（A40、A100、H100、H200）和Intel Ice Lake CPU平台上，通过分子动力学基准（GROMACS、AMBER）和压力测试基准（FIRESTARTER），改变频率缩放和功率上限进行实验。然后提出一个可解释的分析模型，将GPU功率分解为线性和非线性分量，识别工作负载和架构依赖的过渡频率。

Result: 能量-解决方案表现出工作负载和架构依赖的相变；模型拟合经验数据误差低；基线功率、非线性功率行为和过渡频率是主导参数；功率上限效率低于频率降低，尤其对远离TDP的工作负载。

Conclusion: 高效HPC执行是配置依赖问题，存在可识别的相变。通过模型驱动的方法可为不同工作负载和架构选择最优的操作点（频率/功率上限），以实现更高能效。

Abstract: Energy consumption is a key limitation in high-performance computing on heterogeneous CPU-GPU systems. This work studies how hardware configuration affects energy-to-solution under realistic workloads. We study energy efficiency regimes using molecular dynamics benchmarks (GROMACS and AMBER) and a stress-test benchmark (FIRESTARTER) on systems with A40, A100, H100, and H200 GPUs and Intel Ice Lake CPU, varying frequency scaling and power cap. We show that energy-to-solution exhibits workload- and architecture-dependent transitions between efficient and inefficient regimes, driven by nonlinear GPU power-frequency scaling. We introduce an interpretable analytical model that decomposes GPU power into linear and nonlinear components, identifying a workload- and architecture-dependent transition frequency beyond which efficiency degrades. The model fits empirical data with low error and highlights the role of baseline power, nonlinear power behavior, and transition frequency as the dominant parameters governing energy efficiency. Power capping is generally less effective for efficiency tuning than frequency reduction, especially for workloads that operate far from thermal design power. Overall, energy-efficient HPC execution is a configuration-dependent problem with identifiable regime shifts, and we provide model-driven guidance for selecting operating points.

</details>


### [94] [Next-Generation Agentic Reinforcement Learning Systems Enable Self-Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01120)
*Ran Yan,Wei Fu,Jiale Li,Shusheng Xu,Zhiyu Mei,Jiaxuan Gao,Jiarui Zhang,Xujie Shen,Hao Dai,Chuyi He,Zhen Pu,Jun Mei,Zhiyao Lin,Haitao Wang,Zhiqiang Ding,Jiawei Zhang,Huaijie Wang,Ruida Xu,Youhe Jiang,Yi Wu,Tongkai Yang,Binhang Yuan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文指出企业级大规模LLM代理服务面临的核心瓶颈并非强化学习算法本身，而是缺乏支持代理在线RL的系统基础设施，具体体现在三大缺失：标准化轨迹数据协议、企业级数据代理、统一进化控制平面，并提出AReaL2.0作为解决方案实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM代理在部署后处于静态冻结状态，任何改进都需要人工干预的离线流程，而自我进化的代理（如OpenClaw）展示了持续学习的潜力，但这一愿景在企业级大规模部署中受阻。

Method: 通过分析现有代理RL系统的三大不足（缺乏标准化轨迹数据协议、企业级数据代理、统一进化控制平面），提出三个支柱架构，并实例化AReaL2.0——将现有RL基础设施重组为面向代理的在线RL循环，实现从部署工作负载中更新策略权重。

Result: 识别出阻碍企业级自我进化代理部署的关键瓶颈在系统层面而非算法层面，提出三支柱架构设计，并通过AReaL2.0展示具体实现路径。

Conclusion: 下一代代理RL系统必须围绕标准化轨迹协议、企业级数据代理和统一进化控制平面三个支柱进行协同设计，才能实现企业级大规模代理的持续自我进化。

Abstract: LLM agents are rapidly being deployed in production, including coding assistants, customer-support chatbots, and scientific research assistants, yet they remain fundamentally static in enterprise deployment. The LLM weights, system prompts, tool repertoires, and in-context harnesses are frozen at deployment time, and any improvement requires a manual loop of human-curated data collection, offline fine-tuning, modification of the agentic paradigm, and re-deployment. Recent work on self-evolving agents, such as OpenClaw for individual users, indicates that the next leap in agent capability will come from agents that continually learn from their own experience. In this paper, we argue that this vision for self-evolving agent deployment is being held back for enterprise-level large-scale agentic service not by reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms but by agentic online RL systems. Specifically, current agentic RL systems and the surrounding observability software stack are inadequate along three essential aspects: (i) there is no standardized agent trajectory data protocol capable of carrying RL learning signals at step granularity across heterogeneous agent paradigms; (ii) there is no enterprise-grade comprehensive data proxy that converts real workloads into governed learning substrates; and (iii) there is no unified agent evolution control plane that automatically decides, based on trajectory statistics, when to update policy weights or evolve the in-context harness. The next generation of agentic RL systems must be co-designed around these three pillars, and we sketch concrete architectures, case studies, and counter-arguments. We instantiate one branch through AReaL2.0, reorganizing existing RL infrastructure into an agent-oriented online RL loop for policy weight updates from deployed workloads.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [95] [Constructive Alignment: Governing Preference Dynamics in Human-AI Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00001)
*Max Kanwal,Caryn Tran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"建设性对齐"范式，将AI对齐问题从静态偏好满足重新定义为对演化中人类偏好轨迹的控制问题，强调AI系统应治理长期价值形成而非简单满足固定偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI对齐方法将人类偏好视为固定目标进行推断和优化，这与偏好具有层次性、动态性且通过与适应性技术互动构建的实证证据相冲突。随着AI系统越来越持久、个性化和嵌入社会，它们参与塑造人们的注意力、价值观和认可，现有范式无法应对这一情况。

Method: 借鉴行为经济学、心理学和建构主义社会理论，将偏好建模为与环境交互中演化的分层状态变量；采用控制论框架，将系统行为和交互设计视为共同影响世界状态和人类评价状态的变量。

Result: 提出对齐的核心不是控制AI行为，而是规范AI系统如何影响人类偏好的演化——确保价值轨迹保持连贯性、反思性认可、认知基础性、免受操纵边界以及在不确定性下赋权。

Conclusion: 对齐问题从满足静态偏好转变为治理长期价值形成过程，为AI安全研究提供了新的理论视角和形式化框架。

Abstract: Most approaches to AI alignment treat human preferences as fixed targets to be inferred and optimized. This assumption conflicts with extensive empirical evidence showing that preferences are layered, dynamic, and constructed through interaction--particularly with adaptive technologies. As AI systems become more persistent, personalized, and socially embedded, they increasingly participate in shaping what people attend to, value, and endorse over time. We introduce Constructive Alignment, a paradigm that reframes alignment as a control problem over evolving human preference trajectories rather than static preference satisfaction. Drawing on behavioral economics, psychology, and constructivist social theory, we model preferences as layered state variables that evolve under interaction with AI systems. We formalize this view using a control-theoretic framework in which system actions and interaction design jointly influence both world states and human evaluative states. We argue that alignment is not primarily about controlling AI behavior, but about regulating how AI systems influence the evolution of human preferences--ensuring that value trajectories remain coherent, reflectively endorsed, epistemically grounded, bounded against manipulation, and empowering under uncertainty. Alignment thus becomes a problem of governing long-term value formation rather than simply satisfying static preferences.

</details>


### [96] [Bounded Morality: Defining the Space of Moral Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00002)
*Max Kanwal,Caryn Tran,Patrick Mineault*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"有界道德"框架，将赫伯特·西蒙的有界理性思想扩展至道德认知领域，论证有限智能体在道德问题上面临道德广度与道德深度之间的必然权衡，伦理理论是适应不同需求模式的局部高效策略，而非关于道德真理的竞争性主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统道德认知模型将道德推理视为遵循固定伦理理论（义务论、后果主义、美德伦理学）的静态规则或价值函数，忽略了有限智能体的计算资源约束。作者旨在为有限智能体面临的道德问题的计算需求提供形式化分析框架。

Method: 通过两个正交维度形式化道德情境：道德广度（被视为道德相关的实体范围）和道德深度（评估实体间交互所需的推理整合程度）。有限资源在这两个维度之间施加了不可避免的权衡，定义了道德计算的可行空间。

Result: 在该空间中，不同伦理理论对应适应不同需求模式的局部高效策略；框架产生了形式化的道德遗憾和约束下的道德进步概念；人工系统的道德对齐取决于道德推理能力的规模与分配，而非对人类判断的直接模仿。

Conclusion: 有限智能体的道德认知本质上是资源约束下的计算问题，不同伦理理论不是关于道德真理的竞争主张，而是在不同计算需求条件下的适应策略。

Abstract: Moral cognition has traditionally been modeled as adherence to fixed ethical theories--deontology, consequentialism, virtue ethics--implemented as static rules or value functions. We propose Bounded Morality, a formal framework for analyzing the computational demands of moral problems faced by finite agents. Extending Herbert Simon's notion of bounded rationality, we formalize moral situations along two orthogonal dimensions: moral breadth, the scope of entities treated as morally relevant, and moral depth, the inferential integration required to evaluate their interactions. Limited resources impose an unavoidable tradeoff between these dimensions, defining a feasible space of moral computation. Within this space, ethical theories correspond to locally efficient strategies adapted to different demand regimes rather than competing accounts of moral truth. The framework yields a formal notion of moral regret and moral progress under constraint, and implies that moral alignment in artificial systems depends on the scaling and allocation of moral reasoning capacity rather than on direct imitation of human judgments.

</details>


### [97] [The MMM Data Model -- A Normative Specification for Knowledge Interoperability in a Decentralisable Knowledge Commons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00032)
*Mathilde Noual*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MMM数据模型，用于知识文档化，结合少量规范性约束与自由文本标签的灵活性，旨在实现跨学科、跨应用和跨部署的互操作性，而无需语义趋同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信息系统以文档为中心，虽适合大规模分发，但限制了知识的结构化、更新、共享和复用；形式化方法虽解决部分问题，但因优先考虑形式结构而牺牲了人类可用性和适用范围，难以获得广泛采纳；AI系统虽重塑了文档生产，但未能提供传统文档的统一可移植替代方案。

Method: MMM是一个数据模型，从跨学科协作研究的实际需求出发，通过比较信息系统设计空间进行定位。它结合少量规范性约束与自由文本标签的表达自由，设计上支持跨学科、跨应用和跨部署的互操作性，无需语义趋同。

Result: 提供了参考实现和试点部署数据，证明了MMM的可实现性和早期可用性。

Conclusion: MMM在形式化约束与人类可用性之间取得了平衡，为知识文档化提供了一种统一且可移植的替代方案，有望改善跨学科协作中的知识共享与复用。

Abstract: Many information systems are built around documents: self-contained units optimised for print production and linear reading. While effective for large-scale dissemination, the document-centric organisation constrains how knowledge can be structured, updated, shared, and reused. Formal approaches address some of these limitations but struggle to achieve widespread contribution and adoption due to their prioritisation of formal structure over other system properties such as human usability and scope. AI systems are reshaping document production, but without providing a unified portable alternative to traditional documents for humans' expression and exchange of knowledge. This paper presents MMM, a data model for knowledge documentation that emerged from the practical needs of interdisciplinary collaborative research, and positioned here within a comparative analysis of the design space of information systems. MMM combines a small set of normative constraints with the expressive freedom of free-text labels. It is designed for interoperability across disciplines, applications and deployments without requiring semantic convergence. A reference implementation and pilot deployment data demonstrate implementability and early usability.

</details>


### [98] [Making Failure Safe: A Constrained, Verifiable Agent Framework for Open-Web Data Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00035)
*Bo Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种约束性、可验证的智能体框架，将LLM输出从自由形式代码转换为类型化JSON采集器配置，结合六类型采集器分类法、模板与工具函数约束、静态Airflow DAG执行、规则质量检查与结构化反馈修正，在138个任务中验证了框架的有效性，实现了零执行阶段LLM令牌消耗和最低平均时钟时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM和智能体可以从自然语言需求生成网络爬虫，但直接生成因依赖错误、选择器失效、模式不匹配和页面结构异构而不可靠，需要一种更稳定、可验证的方法。

Method: 提出一个约束性、可验证的智能体框架：将LLM输出从自由代码转为类型化JSON采集器配置，包含六类型采集器分类法、模板与工具函数约束、静态Airflow DAG执行、基于规则的质量检查以及结构化反馈修正机制。

Result: 在138个任务上验证了分类法支持基于描述的需类型标注，且稳定实例化需完成源、字段和执行约束；在80个独立源验证任务中，框架实现零执行阶段LLM令牌消耗和最低平均时钟时间。

Conclusion: 该框架以中等的一次性生成质量换取了可复用、确定性、可验证的执行路径，适合重复调度的数据采集任务，定位为可复用、低成本、可验证的开放网络数据采集方案。

Abstract: LLMs and agents can generate web scrapers from natural-language requirements, but direct generation remains unreliable because of dependency errors, broken selectors, schema mismatches, and heterogeneous page structures. We propose a constrained, verifiable agent framework that shifts LLM output from free-form code to typed JSON collector configurations, combining a six-type collector taxonomy, template and utility-function constraints, static Airflow DAG execution, rule-based quality checking, and structured feedback correction. Experiments on 138 tasks show that the taxonomy supports description-based requirement typing, while confirming that stable instantiation requires completing source, field, and execution constraints beyond the initial description. On 80 independently source-verified tasks, the framework runs with zero execution-stage LLM tokens and the lowest average wall-clock time, trading moderate one-shot quality for a reusable, deterministic, and verifiable execution path suited to repeated scheduled collection. These results position the framework as a reusable, low-cost, and verifiable execution path for repeated open-web data collection.

</details>


### [99] [Solution space path planning for supporting en-route air traffic control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00064)
*Yiyuan Zou,Wenying Lyu,Clark Borst*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向航路空中交通管制（ATC）的冲突解脱路径规划算法，该算法以可解释性和计算效率为核心设计原则，集成了三种基于意图的冲突检测方法（距离、时间间隔、区域），并提出顶点搜索（SSPPV）和边搜索（SSPPE）两种变体。实验表明，SSPPV结合区域冲突检测在MUAC Delta扇区场景中平均3.69 ms即可计算出一条无冲突路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路径规划算法与空中交通管制员实际需求之间存在错位，算法设计优先考虑的因素与管制员的操作需要不一致，缺乏兼具可解释性、计算高效性且专为人类使用的决策支持工具。

Method: 基于解空间显示的可解释性和灵活性需求，以及管制员自然应用的决策逻辑（如间隔标准、机动性限制、航路点最小化等），算法集成三种基于意图的冲突检测方法（距离、时间间隔、区域），并提出基于顶点搜索节点（SSPPV）和基于边搜索节点（SSPPE）两种解空间路径规划变体。

Result: SSPPV结合区域冲突检测性能最优，在基于MUAC Delta扇区、5海里网格的运营相关场景中，平均计算时间仅为3.69 ms。

Conclusion: 所提出的算法能够高效、可解释地生成无冲突路径，有望弥合算法设计与管制员实际需求之间的鸿沟，推动路径规划算法在战术管制中的实际采纳。

Abstract: As technology advances, many path-planning algorithms have been proposed for Air Traffic Management, yet their operational adoption in tactical control remains limited, revealing a misalignment between algorithmic design priorities and air traffic controllers' needs. This underscores the need for decision-support solutions that are inherently interpretable, computationally efficient, and explicitly designed for human use. Focusing on this design challenge, this study develops a conflict-free path-planning algorithm for en-route Air Traffic Control (ATC) designed to be compatible with two guiding considerations: (1) the interpretability and flexibility offered by solution-space displays, which motivate constructing an algorithm that exposes all feasible safe actions and accommodates shifting optimization goals; and (2) the decision logic controllers naturally apply when enforcing operational constraints, such as separation standards, maneuverability limits, waypoint minimization, and routing practicality. Centered on these principles, the algorithm integrates three intent-based conflict detection methods -- distance-based, time-interval-based, and zone-based -- within a solution-space framework to identify conflict-free paths in computationally efficient ways. Additionally, vertex-based and edge-based search nodes are proposed for solution space path planning (SSPP), resulting in two variants -- SSPPV and SSPPE, respectively, which are evaluated in terms of computational speed and solution quality. Empirical results show that SSPPV paired with zone-based conflict detection achieves the best performance, computing paths in 3.69 ms on average in operational-relevant scenarios based on the Delta sector of the Maastricht Upper Area Control Centre (MUAC) using a 5 nmi grid.

</details>


### [100] [RareDxR1: Autonomous Medical Reasoning for Rare Disease Diagnosis Beyond Human Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00147)
*Deyang Jiang,Haoran Wu,Ziyi Wang,Yiming Rong,Yunlong Zhao,Ye Jin,Bo Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RareDxR1，一种端到端推理为中心的大语言模型，通过知识内化与自主进化学习框架，直接从非结构化临床笔记进行开放域罕见病诊断，在多个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 罕见病鉴别诊断是一项艰巨的临床任务，现有基于管线的表型提取或检索增强生成方法存在本体预定义导致的信息损失、检索瓶颈以及缺乏诊断逻辑等问题。

Method: 提出渐进式端到端训练框架，结合知识内化与自主进化学习；提出反思增强推理采样（RERS）策略，通过从失败中学习合成专家级诊断轨迹；提出双层次课程强化学习方法逐步掌握罕见病诊断。

Result: RareDxR1在多个基准上取得了最先进的准确率，在开放域罕见病诊断中实现了重大突破。

Conclusion: RareDxR1通过端到端推理和知识内化，有效克服了传统方法的信息丢失和检索瓶颈问题，为开放域罕见病诊断提供了新方案。

Abstract: Rare disease differential diagnosis is a critical yet arduous clinical task, requiring physicians to identify precise phenotypes from complex, unstructured patient symptoms and execute intricate reasoning within a vast search space. However, existing AI approaches typically rely on pipeline-based phenotype extraction or retrieval-augmented generation, which suffer from critical information loss due to predefined ontologies, retrieval bottlenecks, and a lack of diagnostic logic. To address these challenges, we introduce RareDxR1, an end-to-end reasoning-centric large language model designed for open-domain rare disease diagnosis directly from unstructured clinical notes. We design a progressive end-to-end training framework by synergizing knowledge internalization with autonomous evolutionary learning, thereby bypassing reliance on structured phenotypes and closed-set decision-making. To overcome the limitations of RAG and phenotype restriction, we enabled the deep internalization of fragmented rare-disease knowledge directly into the model's parameters. Moreover, to bridge the gap between model generation and expert reasoning, we propose Reflection-Enhanced Reasoning Sampling (RERS), a strategy that synthesizes expert-level diagnostic trajectories by learning from failures without human annotation. Additionally, we propose a dual-level curriculum reinforcement learning approach for gradually mastering rare disease diagnosis. Experimental results demonstrate that RareDxR1 achieves state-of-the-art accuracy across different benchmarks, marking a significant breakthrough in open-domain rare disease diagnosis. Our code and dataset will be publicly available.

</details>


### [101] [A Contextual-Bandit Oversight Game with Two-Sided Informational Asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00155)
*Yunjin Tong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究人类与AI在双向信息不对称下的运行时监督问题，通过引入上下文赌博机团队博弈模型，刻画了“可避免伤害区间”——AI明知有害但短视人类因信任先验而拒绝监督的情形，并分析了该区间在重复博弈中的动态演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的AI系统（如自主机器人）在检查人类无法直接评估的情境时，存在双向信息不对称：人类知道自己的奖励函数，AI知道自身行动的质量。现有CIRL和Oversight Game框架尚未充分刻画这种双向不对称下的监督困境。

Method: 在CIRL和Oversight Game基础上，引入具有双向不对称信息的上下文赌博机团队博弈，包含play/ask/trust/oversee四种行为接口。通过去除物理状态转移，将完整POMDP问题简化为精确的one-shot刻画，同时保留跨轮的共同信念动态控制。

Result: 给出了两种one-shot刻画：团队最优解和短视经验规则。两者之间存在一个“可避免伤害区间”——AI明知行动有害且关闭有帮助，但短视人类因信任先验而拒绝监督。该区间被解释为“非可信监督沟通”的代价。

Conclusion: 可避免伤害区间是监督沟通不可信性的价格；通过被动学习和带一期滞后监督响应的主动信号传递，该区间在重复轮次中可动态消解。

Abstract: We study runtime human oversight of an AI agent when private information runs in both directions: the human privately knows her reward function, while the AI privately knows the quality of the action it proposes. This is the kind of asymmetry that arises naturally when an autonomous robot or software agent has inspected a situation its human supervisor cannot directly assess. Building on Cooperative Inverse Reinforcement Learning (CIRL) and the Oversight Game, we introduce a contextual-bandit team game with two-sided asymmetric information and a play/ask/trust/oversee interface. The bandit structure removes physical state transitions and thereby yields exact one-shot characterizations that would remain conjectural in the full POMDP setting, though the common belief remains a dynamically controlled state across rounds. We give two one-shot characterizations, a team optimum and a behaviorally natural myopic rule, whose gap is a slab of avoidable harm: a region in which the AI privately knows the proposed action is harmful and shutdown would help, yet a myopic human, trusting her prior, declines to oversee. We show this gap is the price of non-credible oversight communication, and give a partial analysis of how it resolves dynamically over repeated rounds through passive learning and active signaling with a one-period-lagged oversight response.

</details>


### [102] [Constructing Epistemic AI Literacy: Detecting Epistemic Aims and Processes in Student-AI Co-Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00211)
*Mengqian Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出Epistemic AI Literacy (EAIL)框架，将AI素养重新概念化为过程导向的认知现象。通过对人机协作编程对话数据的大规模分析，识别出认知目标（掌握导向目标）和认知过程（外包、解释寻求、验证寻求、提示监控、认知论证）的可观察维度。结果显示78.8%的交互缺乏EAIL，仅11.1%的交互表现出高认知参与。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知思维在应用生成式AI的学习过程中起核心作用，但现有AI素养研究缺乏从认知视角理解人机交互中学习过程的框架，特别是在编程情境中学习者需要构建查询、评估AI输出、调节问题解决策略的背景下。

Method: 基于AIR框架（认知目标、理想与可靠认知过程），利用大规模人机协作编程对话数据集，识别并编码认知目标和认知过程的可观察维度，通过量化分析考察学生与GenAI交互中的认知参与模式。

Result: 78.8%的学生-GenAI交互依赖非掌握导向目标及不可靠认知策略（外包、验证寻求）；仅11.1%的交互表现出高认知参与，即掌握导向目标与高级认知策略（如认知论证）相结合。

Conclusion: 学生在使用GenAI时普遍缺乏认知AI素养，多数交互停留在低认知参与水平，需要教育干预以促进更可靠的认知过程和深度学习。

Abstract: Epistemic thinking plays a central role in students' learning processes when applying generative artificial intelligence (GenAI), particularly in programming contexts where learners must construct queries, evaluate and validate AI-generated outputs, and regulate problem-solving strategies. This study introduces the conceptual framework of Epistemic AI Literacy (EAIL), reframing AI literacy as a process-oriented epistemic phenomenon that emerges through dynamic human-AI interactions across different domains. Drawing on the AIR (epistemic aims, ideals and reliable epistemic processes) framework, this study examines how epistemic aims and epistemic processes are enacted in GenAI-supported co-programming activities and explores scalable approaches for operationalizing these constructs in interaction data. Using a large dialogue dataset of human-AI co-programming, this study identifies observable dimensions of epistemic aims (i.e., mastery-oriented aims) and epistemic processes (i.e., outsourcing, explanation seeking, verification seeking, prompt monitoring, and epistemic justification). The results reveal a prevalent lack of EAIL, with 78.8% of student-GenAI interactions relying on non-mastery-oriented aims and less reliable epistemic strategies like outsourcing and verification-seeking. Conversely, only 11.1% of interactions showed high epistemic engagement, where mastery-oriented aims were coupled with advanced epistemic strategies like epistemic justification in a more reliable epistemic process.

</details>


### [103] [From Signals to Structure: How Memory Architecture Drives Language Emergence in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00233)
*Yashar Talebirad,Eden Redman,Ali Parsaee,Osmar R. Zaiane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了刘易斯信号博弈中，不同记忆架构（有无私人笔记本）对LLM智能体从零开始创造共享语言的影响，发现记忆架构比信道容量更重要，拥有持久笔记本的智能体能避免高容量崩溃，实现最可靠的协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在信号博弈中，智能体如何从零开始发明共享语言，特别是记忆架构和信道容量对协调成功的影响，以及现有研究对高容量崩溃现象的解释不足。

Method: 在刘易斯信号博弈框架下，使用LLM智能体，设置五种不同的记忆架构（包括有/无持久笔记本、滚动上下文窗口等）和不同的信道容量配置，通过实验比较各架构下的协调成功率。

Result: 拥有持久私人笔记本的智能体在信道容量=25时达到最佳协调（0.867±0.023）；无状态智能体在中等容量时表现最佳，容量过高时因词汇增长超出上下文窗口追踪能力而崩溃；信息瓶颈理论预测的最佳容量（=8）实际上是脆弱点，而非最优。

Conclusion: 信道容量本身无法预测协调成功与否；记忆架构决定了智能体能否将交互历史转化为稳定的约定；理解信号如何成为语言需要同时考虑信道容量和记忆架构两个维度。

Abstract: How do two agents invent a shared language from scratch? In a Lewis signaling game, a sender and receiver must coordinate on a code using only their interaction history. We study five memory architectures across varying channel configurations with LLM agents and find that memory architecture matters more than channel capacity. Agents with a persistent private notebook benefit from surplus channel capacity and avoid the high-capacity collapse seen in stateless agents, achieving the most reliable coordination ($0.867 \pm 0.023$ at capacity = 25). Stateless agents peak at moderate capacity and then degrade as the vocabulary grows beyond what a rolling context window can track The notebook externalizes learned conventions, freeing agents from having to re-derive codes each round. An information bottleneck-inspired argument predicts an optimal capacity equal to the number of objects. Instead, the bottleneck (capacity = 8) proves to be a fragility point, and surplus capacity is generally better. We show that channel capacity alone cannot predict coordination; memory architecture determines whether agents turn interaction history into stable conventions, and both dimensions are needed to understand how signals become language.

</details>


### [104] [Seed2.0 Model Card: Towards Intelligence Frontier for Real-World Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00248)
*Bytedance Seed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Seed2.0是一个面向复杂真实世界任务的模型系列，通过构建基于用户真实需求的评估体系，重点解决长尾知识和复杂指令跟随两大挑战，在推理、视觉理解和搜索能力上达到世界领先水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别用户的真实需求，构建可靠且前瞻的评估体系，以解决复杂真实世界任务。

Method: 基于用户真实需求和现实复杂场景选择并抽象基准构建评估系统；针对长尾知识和复杂指令跟随两大持久挑战进行改进。

Result: Seed2.0在推理智能、视觉理解和搜索能力上达到世界领先水平，开始展现出处理初始复杂真实世界任务的能力。

Conclusion: Seed2.0朝着解决复杂真实世界任务迈出了有意义的一步，能够为亿万用户提供更大价值。

Abstract: We present Seed2.0, a model series that takes a meaningful step toward solving complex, real-world tasks. Our approach begins with identifying users' genuine needs and constructing a reliable, forward-looking evaluation system by selecting and abstracting benchmarks grounded in these needs and in realistic, complex scenarios. Guided by this evaluation system, Seed2.0 targets two persistent challenges, long-tail knowledge and complex instruction following, substantially improving the model's reliability on intricate, long-horizon tasks. Beyond these, Seed2.0 delivers world-leading reasoning intelligence, visual understanding, and search capabilities that address the most common needs of a broad user base. Through extensive real-world use cases documented in this model card, we demonstrate that Seed2.0 begins to exhibit the ability to handle initial complex real-world tasks, delivering greater value to hundreds of millions of users.

</details>


### [105] [Mnemosyne: Agentic Transaction Processing for Validating and Repairing AI-generated Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00269)
*Edward Y. Chang,Longling Geng,Emily J. Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic事务处理（ATP）模型，将生成式动作视为不可信提案，仅当其通过声明性约束集C的确定性准入后才被运行时提交。在Mnemosyne运行时中实现，证明四项安全属性，并通过实验验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM、求解器和智能体团队生成的行动计划可能语法有效但存在过时、不可行、冲突或破坏证据等问题，缺乏事务性保证。

Method: 提出ATP事务模型，核心原则：提案非真理，提案无法预见所有干扰；任何实体可提案但仅运行时负责准入和提交；遇到意外干扰时在边界内反应式修复而非信任新提案。在Mnemosyne中实现，包含仅追加转换日志、有效状态投影、依赖安全补偿和活跃提交记录。

Result: 在九项伪造测试中拒绝目标违规同时接受有效工作；投影与验证开销低于6%；局部修复编辑操作数量比全局重算少一个数量级。

Conclusion: ATP将提案层与运行时层分离，使已提交状态的正确性独立于提案层的能力或诚实性，为生成式动作提供可证明的事务安全保证。

Abstract: LLMs, solvers, and agent teams increasingly generate workflow actions, repairs, and plans, but a generated action may be syntactically valid yet stale, infeasible, conflicting, or destructive of the evidence that triggered a repair. We introduce Agentic Transaction Processing (ATP), a transaction model that treats generated actions as untrusted proposals until they pass deterministic admission under a declared, executable constraint set C. The principle is two-sided: a proposal is not truth, and no proposal foresees every disruption: anything may propose, but only the runtime admits and commits, and when an unforeseen disruption strikes it repairs reactively within bounds rather than trusting a fresh proposal. Relative to C, committed-state correctness becomes independent of the competence, honesty, or learning of the proposing layer. We realize ATP in Mnemosyne, a runtime with an append-only transition log, effective-state projection, dependency-safe compensation, and active commitment records, and prove four safety properties relative to C (authority separation, serial-equivalent generative admission, evidence-preserving repair, and obligation containment) together with a bounded-reactive-repair guarantee for its localized repair protocol (LCRP). A reproducible artifact rejects the targeted violations across nine falsification tests while still admitting valid work, at under 6% projection-and-validation overhead, and bounded local repair edits an order of magnitude fewer operations than global recompute. Mnemosyne is open source: https://github.com/eyuchang/Mnemosyne/tree/arxiv-atp-rq1-rq9b-r8-v2.

</details>


### [106] [Managed Autonomy at Runtime: Gear-Based Safety and Governance for Single- and Multi-Agent Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00334)
*Srini Ramaswamy,Wang Miaosheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为\system{}的离散时间控制系统，通过五个执行档位（观察、建议、规划、执行、交互）与效用门控调度及事件驱动回退相结合，为单智能体证明单调稳定性、执行安全性、最终稳定性、回退完备性及档位约束MDP等价性；为多智能体CPS系统结合\smart{}托管自治生命周期，共识门控、群体李雅普诺夫分析、智能体档位权限和会合控制提供分布式安全保障。在三台UR5机器人装配单元上的10,000次蒙特卡洛评估实现了99.6%的异常检测率，检测延迟降低3.5倍，并提供形式化物理工作空间安全证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体（LLM驱动或机器人）在无持续人类监督时面临三类共同故障模式：未验证动作导致的安全违规、无约束循环导致的行为不稳定性、未处理错误状态导致的连续性损失。现有方法缺乏统一的轻量级运行时控制机制来同时应对这些问题。

Method: 提出\system{}系统：(1) 单智能体：五个执行档位（\Gobs{}、\Gsug{}、\Gplan{}、\Gexec{}、\Gint{}）配合效用门控调度和事件驱动回退，并证明其单调稳定性、执行安全性、最终稳定性、回退完备性，以及等价于档位约束MDP；(2) 多智能体CPS：结合\smart{}托管自治生命周期（四个治理状态：稳定/元/辅助/受控），引入共识门控、群体李雅普诺夫分析、智能体档位权限和会合控制，提供分布式安全保障（零碰撞）。

Result: 在三台UR5机器人装配单元上，基于NIST机器人臂位置精度退化测量数据集的蒙特卡洛评估（10,000次）中，\system{}实现99.6%的异常检测率（对比单智能体基线2.1%），检测延迟降低3.5倍，并提供形式化物理工作空间安全证书。

Conclusion: 执行档位作为\smart{}运行时治理状态下的微级权限机制，将动作控制与自治治理相分离，有效解决了自主智能体在缺乏人类监督时的安全、稳定和连续性三大故障模式。

Abstract: Autonomous agents, whether LLM-driven software agents or robotic physical agents, face a common class of failure modes when operating without continuous human oversight: safety violations from unverified actions, behavioral instability from unconstrained loops, and continuity loss from unhandled error states. We develop \system{}, a discrete-time control system that combines five execution gears (\Gobs{}, \Gsug{}, \Gplan{}, \Gexec{}, \Gint{}) with utility-gated dispatch and event-driven fallback. For the single-agent case, we prove monotonic stability, execution safety, eventual stabilization, fallback completeness, and equivalence to a gear-constrained Markov decision process. For multi-agent cyber-physical systems (CPS), we apply the established \smart{} managed-autonomy lifecycle and map runtime evidence into its four governance states (\Stable{}/\Meta{}/\Assisted{}/\Regulated{}). Consensus gating, swarm-level Lyapunov analysis, per-agent gear authority, and rendezvous control provide distributed safety and stability guarantees, including zero collision under the stated assumptions. We evaluate the resulting runtime on a three-agent UR5 robotic assembly cell using fault magnitudes calibrated from the NIST \emph{Degradation Measurement of Robot Arm Position Accuracy} dataset across 10,000 Monte Carlo episodes. It achieves a 99.6\% anomaly detection rate versus 2.1\% for the single-agent baseline, reduces detection latency by $3.5\times$, and supplies a formal physical-workspace safety certificate. The execution gears act as micro-level permissions beneath the \smart{} runtime governance states, separating action control from autonomy governance.

</details>


### [107] [Personalization as Inverse Planning: Learning Latent Design Intents for Agentic Slide Generation via Structural Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00407)
*Tianci Liu,Zihan Dong,Linjun Zhang,Haoyu Wang,jing Gao,Emre Kiciman,Ranveer Chandra,Wei-Ting Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SPIRE框架，通过结构去噪和强化学习多智能体协作，解决页面级幻灯片个性化设计中的隐式设计意图捕捉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体方法依赖预设模板或用户详细指令，难以捕捉细粒度页面级设计的隐式意图，导致页面级幻灯片个性化问题未得到解决。

Method: 将PSP形式化为逆规划问题，通过故意破坏干净幻灯片的视觉结构创建可验证的去噪任务，两个智能体通过强化学习协作优化可执行设计。证明了结构去噪是PSP的一致性替代，且多智能体公式可严格降低策略梯度方差。

Result: 大量实验证明了SPIRE的优越性。

Conclusion: SPIRE通过结构去噪作为一致性替代方案，结合多智能体强化学习，有效解决了无需预知具体执行工具的页面级幻灯片个性化设计问题。

Abstract: Slide design requires personalizing both deck themes and page layouts. Yet, current AI agent-based methods struggle with fine-grained, page-level design. Solely relying on prespecified templates or user verbose instructions, they fail to capture latent design intents, leaving Page-level Slide Personalization (PSP) unresolved. To close this gap, this work formulates PSP as an inverse planning problem. We propose to learn a design intent without assuming any knowledge of the specific executing tools (e.g., PowerPoint, Beamer) being used. However, relinquishing control over these tools makes the problem intractable to optimize end-to-end. To overcome this, we propose SPIRE, a principled framework to solve PSP approximately. By intentionally corrupting the visual structures of clean slides, SPIRE creates a verifiable task to denoise the corruption, whereby two agents learn to collaboratively refine executable designs via reinforcement learning (RL). We present a proof that structural denoising is a consistent surrogate for PSP, and that the multi-agent formulation strictly reduces policy gradient variance in RL. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of SPIRE.

</details>


### [108] [PHREEQC-MCQ-200: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Tool-Augmented Scientific Simulator Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00436)
*Ke Zhang,Sahchit Chundur,Mohammad Javad Qomi,Maziar Raissi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PHREEQC-MCQ-200基准，用于评估工具增强型大语言模型在水文地球化学模拟中的可靠性，发现模拟器访问虽提升整体准确率但存在非单调性退化，并揭示了输出访问协议对模型性能的关键影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型智能体越来越多地接入科学软件，但尚不清楚工具接入何时真正提升科学计算的可靠性而非增加复杂性，缺乏系统性的评估基准来诊断这一问题。

Method: 构建PHREEQC-MCQ-200基准（200道多选题，源自21个经验证的PHREEQC场景），评估工具增强型智能体在确定性水文地球化学模拟中的端到端表现，包括构建输入、执行模拟、检查输出和给出最终答案的全流程。

Result: 模拟器访问显著提升了整体准确率，但增益非单调——工具增强型智能体会丢失原本无工具时能正确回答的题目；输出访问协议影响显著，目录式接口可降低token成本并保持/提升强模型准确率，但会降低中等模型的性能。

Conclusion: 科学工具使用应被视为端到端的诊断问题而非简单的工具调用能力，评估应报告准确率、项目级保留率、输出访问敏感性、轨迹失败和计算链断裂位置等多维指标。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly connected to scientific software, yet it remains unclear when tool access makes scientific computation more reliable rather than merely more complex. We introduce PHREEQC-MCQ-200, a benchmark for evaluating tool-augmented agents on deterministic aqueous-geochemistry simulations. The benchmark contains 200 multiple-choice questions derived from 21 validated PHREEQC scenarios, requiring agents to construct simulator inputs, execute PHREEQC, inspect structured outputs, and commit to final answers.
  Across multiple frontier and mid-tier model families, simulator access substantially improves aggregate accuracy, confirming that grounded execution is necessary for many scientific-computation tasks. However, the gains are not monotonic: tool-augmented agents also lose items they answered correctly without tools, revealing regressions that average accuracy alone hides. We further show that output-access protocol matters. A table-of-contents interface can reduce token cost while preserving or improving accuracy for stronger models, but it degrades performance for mid-tier models that cannot reliably navigate structured simulator outputs.
  PHREEQC-MCQ-200 therefore frames scientific tool use as an end-to-end diagnostic problem rather than a simple tool-calling capability. We argue that evaluations of scientific agents should report not only accuracy, but also item-level retention, output-access sensitivity, trajectory failures, and where the computation chain breaks.

</details>


### [109] [Agri-SAGE: Simulation-Grounded Multi-Agent LLM for Context-Aware Agricultural Advisory Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00454)
*Vedant Balasubramaniam,Geetha Charan,Manojkumar Patil,Rohit P Suresh,V Priyanka,Kodur Sai Vinay Sathvik,Y. Narahari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Agri-SAGE是一个将检索增强的多智能体LLM推理与APSIM生物物理模拟相结合的闭环框架，用于生成和验证农艺建议。实验表明，Tree of Thoughts实现最高产量，Reflexion在较低计算成本下达到相当效果，三者均显著优于静态基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有农艺咨询系统面临双重困境：静态指南忽略季节内变异和动态不确定性，而LLM驱动的系统可能给出农艺上可信但生理上不合理的建议。作者旨在解决这两个局限性。

Method: 提出Agri-SAGE闭环框架，集成检索增强的多智能体LLM推理与APSIM生物物理模拟。评估三种推理策略：Plan-and-Solve、Tree of Thoughts和Reflexion，基于10年回顾性分析。

Result: 三种方法均显著优于静态Package-of-Practice基线。Tree of Thoughts实现峰值产量最优；Reflexion通过跨季节情景记忆在较低计算成本下达到相当的农艺效果。

Conclusion: Agri-SAGE框架有效结合LLM推理与物理模拟，解决了静态指南和纯LLM方法的各自局限。Reflexion在效率和效果之间实现了良好平衡。

Abstract: Agricultural advisory systems face a fundamental tension: static agronomic guidelines offer consistent, evidence-based recommendations, yet remain blind to in-season variability and dynamic uncertainties. Recent advisory systems powered by LLMs are liable for a different risk of generating recommendations that are agronomically credible but physiologically unconvincing. Agri-SAGE is a closed-loop framework designed to resolve the above two limitations by integrating retrieval-grounded multi-agent LLM reasoning with APSIM-based biophysical simulation, to generate and validate agronomic advisories. To assess this framework, we evaluate three reasoning approaches, namely Plan-and-Solve, Tree of Thoughts, and Reflexion, over a 10-year retrospective analysis. All three significantly outperform static PoP (Package-of-Practice) baselines, with Tree of Thoughts achieving impressive peak yields. At the same time, Reflexion achieves comparable agronomic outcomes at substantially lower computational cost by leveraging cross-seasonal episodic memory.

</details>


### [110] [Multi-scale Mixture of World Models for Embodied Agents in Evolving Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00457)
*Jinwoo Jang,Daniel J. Rho,Sihyung Yoon,Hyunsuk Cho,Honguk Woo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MuSix通过尺度感知的世界模型混合与演化机制，解决具身智能体中MoE缺乏显式尺度路由和统一更新策略的问题，在EmbodiedBench和HAZARD上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体需要在条件变化时进行多尺度推理和知识适应，但现有MoE方法存在两个关键挑战：1）路由缺乏显式的尺度概念，无法在特定尺度上进行针对性更新；2）统一更新策略无法适应各尺度知识过时的不同速率。

Method: 提出MuSix框架，包含两阶段路由机制和尺度依赖的演化策略。路由方面：元路由器基于解释水平理论中的体验距离将情境新颖性映射到连续尺度空间的权重，然后各尺度的基础路由器在识别出的尺度内选择世界模型。演化方面：尺度依赖的遗忘率使低尺度知识快速刷新而高尺度抽象持续保持，门控跨尺度迁移维护层次间的一致性。

Result: 在EmbodiedBench和HAZARD基准测试上，MuSix在多尺度推理和动态适应任务中优于所有基线方法。

Conclusion: MuSix通过尺度感知的世界模型混合与演化有效解决了具身智能体中的多尺度推理和动态适应问题，验证了解释水平理论在路由机制中的应用可行性。

Abstract: Embodied agents operating in the real world require multi-scale reasoning and knowledge adaptation as conditions change. We identify two challenges in applying Mixture of Experts (MoE) to this setting: routing lacks an explicit notion of scale, preventing targeted updates at specific scales, and a uniform update policy cannot accommodate the different rates at which knowledge at each scale becomes outdated. We present MuSix, a framework that addresses both challenges through scale-aware world model mixture and evolution. A two-stage routing mechanism grounds scale selection in experiential distance, a measure of situational novelty inspired by Construal Level Theory: a meta-router first maps this quantity to a weight over continuous scale space, then per-scale base routers select world models within the identified scale. For adaptation, scale-dependent forgetting rates allow low-scale knowledge to refresh rapidly while high-scale abstractions persist, and gated inter-scale transfer maintains coherence across the hierarchy. Experiments on EmbodiedBench and HAZARD show that MuSix improves over state-of-the-art baselines on multi-scale reasoning and dynamic adaptation.

</details>


### [111] [AI Native Games: A Survey and Roadmap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00527)
*Zhiyue Xu,Fandi Meng,Kaijie Xu,Clark Verbrugge,Simon Lucas,Jian Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了"AI原生游戏"的定义标准：运行时生成式AI构成核心玩法循环，若移除AI组件则游戏核心玩法崩溃或根本改变。基于此定义筛选出53款AI原生游戏/原型，提出G/N双轴分类法，并指出核心设计问题在于将语义开放性组织为稳定游戏玩法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成式AI在游戏中的应用日益增多，但"AI原生游戏"缺乏明确定义，难以区分哪些游戏真正以AI为核心玩法机制，哪些只是AI增强或辅助。需要建立严格标准来界定和分类AI原生游戏。

Method: 提出反事实判断标准（移除AI组件后核心玩法是否崩溃），基于此筛选53款候选游戏/原型，建立G/N双轴分类法（G轴：面向玩家的游戏类型；N轴：使AI不可或缺的主导AI机制），并对语料库进行系统分析。

Result: 发现现有AI原生游戏集中在语言导向设计（叙事冒险、认知交互、生成式叙事），而语义裁决、多智能体模拟、生成式建造、关系/同伴玩法等类别代表性不足。核心设计问题是将语义开放性组织为稳定游戏玩法。

Conclusion: AI原生设计依赖于机械不变性（目标、规则、状态、反馈、节奏、玩家能动性），使开放式的AI输出变得可解释和有意义。提出可控生成、AI即机制设计、多模态/多智能体系统、推理经济学、评估、安全与监管等未来路线图。

Abstract: Generative AI now enables games to produce dialogue, quests, characters, images, and worlds at runtime. Yet generation alone does not make a game AI-native, nor does it guarantee playability. This paper defines AI-native games by whether runtime generative AI is constitutive of the core loop: if the AI component were removed or trivially replaced, the central form of play would collapse or become fundamentally different. This counterfactual criterion separates AI-native games from AI-augmented games, boundary artifacts, chatbots, tavern-style role-play, procedural content generation, and AI-assisted production. Using this definition, we screen candidate artifacts and analyze 53 publicly available AI-native games and prototypes. We introduce a dual-axis G/N taxonomy: the G-axis captures player-facing game type, while the N-axis captures the dominant AI mechanic that makes generative AI indispensable to play. The corpus is concentrated around language-forward designs, especially narrative adventure, epistemic interaction, and generative narrative, while categories such as semantic adjudication, multi-agent simulation, generative construction, and relationship/companion play remain less represented. We argue that the central design problem is organizing semantic openness into stable gameplay. AI-native design depends on mechanical invariants: goals, rules, state, feedback, pacing, and player agency that make open-ended AI outputs interpretable and consequential. We conclude with a roadmap for controllable generation, AI-as-mechanic design, multimodal and multi-agent systems, inference economics, evaluation, safety, and regulation.

</details>


### [112] [HARC: Coupling Harmfulness and Refusal Directions for Robust Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00572)
*Shei Pern Chua,Fangzhao Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现对齐LLM中安全表示的方向性机制：遗忘和拒绝方向存在于残差流中。越狱通过抑制这些方向实现。提出HARC方法，在提示和响应位置耦合这两个方向进行微调，在不损害通用能力的情况下增强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解对齐LLM如何内部表示安全性对于诊断对齐漏洞至关重要，因为它能解释越狱为何成功，并为设计稳健的对齐策略提供信息。

Method: 1) 分析显示越狱通过抑制拒绝或危害方向来实现提示编码攻击，不同攻击类别在危害-拒绝平面占据可分离区域。2) 扩展分析到响应位置，发现模型在生成内容时能识别危害性，即使提示侧未识别。3) 提出HARC（危害-拒绝耦合）微调方法，配对提示和响应位置的这两个方向进行干预。

Result: HARC不损害通用能力或增加过度拒绝。在六个基线方法中实现了最强的鲁棒性-能力-可用性权衡。危害和拒绝方向在五个模型家族和两个规模上可迁移，无需架构特定的调优。

Conclusion: HARC方法通过限制干预到危害-拒绝子空间，保持残差流其余部分完好，在不降低通用能力或过度拒绝的情况下提升安全性，且具有跨模型迁移性。

Abstract: Understanding how aligned LLMs internally represent safety is critical for diagnosing alignment vulnerabilities, as it explains why jailbreaks succeed and informs the design of robust alignment strategies. Prior work shows that aligned LLMs encode harmfulness and refusal as separable directions in the residual stream at prompt-side token positions. We show that jailbreaks succeed at prompt encoding by suppressing either the refusal or harmfulness direction before any token is generated, with distinct attack classes occupying separable regions of the harmfulness-refusal plane. Extending the analysis to response-token positions, we find that the model recognizes harmful content while it is generating that content, even when it failed to recognize the input as harmful at the prompt side. Motivated by our findings, we introduce HARC (Harmfulness-And-Refusal Coupling), a fine-tuning method that pairs the two directions across both prompt and response positions. Since the intervention is confined to the harmfulness-refusal subspace, it leaves the rest of the residual stream intact and does not degrade general capability or inflate over-refusal. Across extensive experiments, HARC achieves the strongest robustness-capability-usability trade-off among six baselines spanning the major training-time and inference-time safety methods. The harmfulness and refusal directions at prompt and response positions transfer across the five model families and two scales we tested without architecture-specific tuning.

</details>


### [113] [AGI Maze as a Benchmark Framework for World-Modeling Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00627)
*Alexey Potapov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AGI Maze框架，用于构建需要世界状态表征的迷宫任务。评估表明，普通LLM无法在推理时内部表征迷宫，即使允许使用消息历史作为工作记忆，LLM智能体仍无法可靠解决小型迷宫。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM默认的下一token预测模式无法产生持久、可操作的外部世界表征，在部分可观测、有状态且需要记忆和隐藏状态结构化假设的环境中表现不佳。现有基准缺乏对智能体学习并使用世界状态表征能力的评估。

Method: 提出AGI Maze框架，提供一系列基于网格的迷宫任务，包含简洁API和多种难度级别。在该框架上评估了多种普通LLM在简单迷宫上的表现，并引入一个允许使用消息历史作为工作记忆的基线智能体。

Result: 普通LLM在推理时无法内部表征迷宫结构；允许使用消息历史的基线智能体虽然性能有所提升，但仍无法在比人类所需更多的时间步内可靠解决小型迷宫。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在需要显式世界状态建模和持久表征的任务上存在根本性局限，AGI Maze框架可作为评估和推动这一能力的有效基准。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are powerful pattern-completion systems, but their default operating mode - predicting the next token from a static context - does not reliably produce persistent, manipulable representations of an external world.
